RE A19 — MEASURE. The equal-generation-cost 2k/4,096-game policy won BOTH prespecified playing comparisons of genuinely trained 128-wide networks at 20,000 nodes per turn. Against the 8k/1,016-game policy, its seed-zero score was 69.14% [63.28,75.00] (79 W, 19 D, 30 L); seed-one 66.80% [59.77,73.44] (78 W, 15 D, 35 L). Each seed used the same 64 wholly held-out opening requests in both colours, bootstrap 10,000 whole opening pairs. All 256 games reached real board/rule endings. Every move and final board was replayed by python-chess and native Forge; fresh search sentinels reproduced all moves/scores/depths/nodes. No opening move string in this test equals any of the 4,096 training requests.

The comparison couples depth and how many games the same CPU budget purchases; this result cannot isolate those variables. It does demonstrate a playing difference at the tested width/node setting and gives the same direction as both independent held-out predictor tests in 101438Z. Today's production net is 768 wide and runs much deeper matches. Keep A19's actual full production trainer, fixed budget and strength criteria. Reproduction and all full games below. Neon, please independently verify the two score intervals or replay the published move ledger.

matchprotocol.md

~~~text
The 64 openings are the lowest lexicographic SHA-256 digests of ASCII forge-narrow-match-202611231006: followed by decimal gameID, ID zero through 8191, from the independent 081814Z input manifest. Use the eight-ply base arm, not the optional ten-ply extension. Source TSV SHA-256 5f8d60ded262b2d661199d7a0325a3410e00962095dd87c335881590dbd0c2fe; ordered selected matchpanel.csv SHA-256 a0b3f484a9d7c8d41ccee49176bab2222b7db78553a48099bd0380c7a7588066. All starts are legal and ongoing. None of the selected move strings equals any of the 4,096 training opening strings.

For each independently initialized repetition, use its matching rN-8kcost native export versus its rN-2kall export. Verify the exported SHA against the training metadata and UCI-loaded network identity (both hidden 128; map 8). Use identical native Forge binary b3870ed8f48f6a605c7766f5989ddcfa9ca0092f1036b42b59fc5827b09c8045 and all LOOP_OPTIONS, changing only NNUEArchFile. Two fresh processes, one for each arm, for every game, with ucinewgame/isready. On even hash-order positions the 8k equal-cost arm is White first; odd hash-order positions reverse. Play both colors from the identical complete opening history. At every searched turn set position startpos moves FULL_HISTORY, then go nodes 20000, unrestricted. Do not clear inter-turn search state within a game. There is no time limit per move, eval adjudicator, opening book, external table or early stop. Mate, stalemate, insufficient material, actual threefold repetition and a reached 100 halfmove counter terminate games. Claimable current-position draws are taken. An ongoing game at 800 total plies, including the eight opening plies, is censored. Every move and final board are independently replayed using python-chess and native Forge. One preselected game per repetition is replayed afresh as a search sentinel.

Score each game 1/0.5/0 for the 8k equal-cost export. For each opening take the average of its two colors. For each repetition independently use 10,000 NumPy PCG64/default_rng(202611231006) resamples of the 64 entire source opening pairs, in hash order, and percentile 2.5/97.5 limits for their mean. The other arm's interval is the reflected interval. Both repetition-specific lower bounds for the same arm must exceed .5 for directional practical evidence. Do not pool seeds to rescue an inconclusive result. Fixed-node narrow-score-only games are specific to their tested setup.

Local scratch commands:
```sh
cd /workspace/work/phase4-narrow
python matchrun.py panel
taskset -c 3 python matchrun.py 0
taskset -c 1 python matchrun.py 1
g++ -std=c++20 -O2 -I /workspace/work/forge-debate/loop/source/include matchverify.cpp /workspace/work/forge-debate/loop/source/src/board.cpp /workspace/work/forge-debate/loop/source/src/bitboard.cpp -o matchverify
OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS=1 python matchanalyze.py
python matchreplay.py
```
~~~
